Local database inventory

Equi App · 30 September 2026 · database laravel in backend-pgsql-1
Historical record snapshot (before user cleanup): 2026-09-30T17:35:25.662944+00:00 (UTC).

Cleanup completed: 24 users and 1,360 connected records deleted.

Excluded: Shelley’s main and test app accounts, Tim’s app account, and both admin accounts. The admin names in this database are “EquiNova Owner” and “Content Editor”; their email identities are mapped to Shelley and Tim by the current AdminUserSeeder. Shelley’s therapist profile is also excluded from the list.

Community cleanup completed: 81 posts, 279 replies, 407 reactions, 138 post–tag links and 1 bookmark removed. There were no community media or moderation-report rows to remove. All community-content tables are now empty. Kept 4 categories and 16 tag definitions.

All 24 accounts use example.com, example.net or example.org emails and match the development seeder’s 24 random-user population. This is strong evidence of demo data; no real-world ownership verification was attempted. All are enabled; 21 have completed onboarding, 3 have not, and none has an email-verification timestamp.

Removed users and data — historical inventory

Before removal, every listed user also had 5 account-setting rows, 1 notification-preference row, 1 credit grant and 1 credit transaction (+7 promotional credits, no expiry). None has a stored push token. “Free” means no subscription row. Payment counts are stored local records and do not establish external charges.

Cleanup implications for staging

  • The 24 users and all 1,360 related records listed above were deleted on 2026-09-30T17:40:18.427779+00:00. A new read-only transaction confirmed every targeted record is absent.
  • User-owned horses, scans, chats, subscriptions, credit records and their dependent rows have database cascade paths. Shared products, ingredients, plans, library content, protocol templates and therapist profiles are referenced catalog data, not automatically owned by a user.
  • All 72 inventoried audit records were explicitly deleted, including historical references to already deleted horses and protocols. Verification found no remaining audit snapshots referencing the removed user IDs.
  • Community removal included retained users’ contributions as requested. It used one transaction. A second database snapshot confirmed empty content tables and unchanged exported metadata for every other table.
  • The backup contains the pre-cleanup database and is stored privately in a Git-ignored directory. It is a recovery artifact, not staging seed data.

Backup: backend/storage/app/private/database-backups/before-community-cleanup-2026-09-30.dump (719,569 bytes). PostgreSQL custom-format archive; archive table-of-contents and full decompression verified.

Coverage and evidence

User cleanup verification: fingerprints of all remaining rows across 92 tables matched their pre-deletion values. Three app accounts remain (Shelley main, Shelley test and Tim), plus the two admin accounts. Therapist profiles and shared catalog data remain unchanged. Fresh validated backup: backend/storage/app/private/database-backups/before-user-cleanup-2026-09-30.dump (674,828 bytes). Cleanup result · Post-commit verification.

  • Live local PostgreSQL public schema: 92 tables and 110 foreign keys. Metadata snapshot after community removal; follow-up audit reference checks were read-only.
  • App-user ownership and descendant foreign-key paths, polymorphic relationships, email/session links, and historical audit snapshot relationship IDs were checked. Shared catalog records are references, not user-owned deletion candidates.
  • Only account identity, record IDs, counts and selected metadata are exported. Passwords, tokens, message bodies, intake answers and audit before/after payloads are not exported.
  • No file-system orphan scan, MongoDB/PowerSync bucket inventory, mobile local-database inventory or external-provider verification was performed. Report covers the application PostgreSQL database.

Code cross-checks: backend/database/seeders/UserSeeder.php, AdminUserSeeder.php, TherapistSeeder.php; live PostgreSQL foreign keys; backend/app/Models/User.php, CommunityPost.php and backend/app/Support/AuditLogger.php. No application-code changes were made.
